Ur tesεiḍ asireg i waya.
Hwan tiseddaṛin.
Tḥeḍrem i temḍelt n Tom ?
Ɣur Ṭawes, d amɣar n taddart.
Teεyiḍ si tudert?
Ur fqiɛeɣ ara akk fell-akent.
Ur εad nesεi ayen ilaqen d isallen akken neṭṭef rray ɣef aya.
Tom yessuden yelli-s n εemmi-s.
Yesεa akayad ssmana i d-iteddun.
Nekk briɣ.
Ḥmida yettmeslay s teqbaylit.
Iṭebbeg-iyi Tom.
Lhiɣ, tanemirt imi i d-tesutreḍ!
D ayen i sεiɣ d ṣṣerf.
Mačči εemda i t-nɣiɣ.
Ur tettuy ara ad tmeyyzeḍ uqbel ad d-tmeslayeḍ.
Amulli ameggaz a Muiriel!
Texdem-d Marie tiɣṛifin ass-a.
Meḥsub kifkif.
Urar d ddeɛwessu.
Tlemdemt Leqran.
Amek i s-qqaren i umedyaz-nni analman i ssnen merra warrac uɣerbaz ?
Bɣiɣ ad steɛfuɣ kra.
Ḥsissen yella di temdint.
Ḥemmleɣ m'ara tcennuḍ.
Ḥemleɣ tamurt-iw.
Ahya Leqbayel, kecmet ɣer ublug-a teɣrem taqbaylit.
Ur k-ittaǧǧa ara Tom ad tṛuḥeḍ.
Nudaɣ ad ẓreɣ ugar ɣef wergaz-agi.
Tεawen baba-s deg tebḥirt.
Ţamaziɣt d tutlayt-nneɣ.
Tuɣal mecḥuṛet.
Ṣaliḥ yessen ad iger.
Meslay ɣef lmalayek ad tesleḍ i uḥbak n wafriwen-nsent.
Ccrab d tamedyezt deg tqerεet.
Itteɛṛaḍ.
Atan tura qrib ad tejjiḍ.
